    function GB_ga_getCookie(name){
        var value = "; " + document.cookie;
        var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
            return decodeURIComponent(parts.pop().split(";").shift())
        } else {
            return '';
        }
    }
   ;(function (i, s, o, g, r, a, m) {
      i['GoogleAnalyticsObject'] = r;
      i[r] = i[r] || function () {
        (i[r].q = i[r].q || []).push(arguments)
      }, i[r].l = 1 * new Date();
      a = s.createElement(o),
        m = s.getElementsByTagName(o)[0];
      a.async = 1;
      a.src = g;
      m.parentNode.insertBefore(a, m)

  var _gaGetQueryString = function(name) {
    //get url query params
    var reg = new RegExp("(^|&)" + name + "=([^&]*)(&|$)", "i");
    var r = window.location.search.substr(1).match(reg);
    if (r != null) return unescape(r[2]); return null;
  }

  window.GB_ga_currentRate = Number('23201') || 1
  window.GB_ga_transformPrice = function(val) {
		if(GB_ga_currentRate) {
		    return (val/GB_ga_currentRate).toFixed(2)
		} else {
		    return val
		}
  }

  function GBGaSend(params) {
    var payload = ['shein.send', 'event']
    for (var key in params) {
      if (typeof params[key] !== 'undefined') {
        payload.push(params[key])
      }
      if (key == 'beacon') {
        payload.push({ transport: 'beacon' })
      }
    }
    ga.apply(null, payload)
  }

<script>
    // 环境变量 localhost/debug/gray/production
    var NODE_SERVER_ENV = 'production';

    var ErrorJs = (function(global) {
        if(global.ErrorJs) return global.ErrorJs;

        var _errorMap = {}; // 存储错误id，判断是否是重复上报
        var _config = {
            id: 0, // id, 若没有id不会上报
            url: 'https://www.srmdata.com/unusual', // 上报 接口
            ignore: [], // 忽略某个错误, 支持 Regexp 和 Function
            random: 1, // 抽样 0-1 1为全量
            delay: 1000, // 延迟上报
            repeat: 5, // 重复上报次数(对于同一个错误超过多少次不上报),
            hostnameFilter: ['shein.com', 'shein.co', 'shein.in', 'shein.tw', 'shein.se', 'sheinoutlet.com', 'emmacloth.com', 'makemechic.com', 'romwe.com', 'romwe.co'],
            envFilter: ['localhost', 'debug', 'gray']
        };

        var E = {
            extend: function(target, source) {
                for(var key in source) {
                    target[key] = source[key];
                }
                return target;
            },
            isObject: function(value) {
                return Object.prototype.toString.call(value) === '[object Object]';
            },
            checkType: function(value, type) {
                return Object.prototype.toString.call(value) === '[object ' + type + ']';
            },
            // 判断重复次数是否超过配置的次数
            isRepeat: function(id) {
                if(!id) return false;
                var times = _errorMap[id] = (parseInt(_errorMap[id], 10) || 0) + 1;
                return times > _config.repeat;
            },
            isRandomPassed: function() {
                return Math.random() >= _config.random;
            },
            // 根据ignore配置项过滤掉不需要上报的错误
            shouldIgnore: function(msg) {
                var ignoreConf = _config.ignore;
                return ignoreConf.some(function(value) {
                    return (E.checkType(value, 'RegExp') && value.test(msg)) || (E.checkType(value, 'Function') && value(msg));
                })
            },
            filterEnvAndHost: function() {
                var hostname = '';
                if(global.location) hostname = global.location.hostname;
                // 本地/测试/灰度环境不上报，不在监控域名内的不上报
                var envPass = _config.envFilter.indexOf(NODE_SERVER_ENV) === -1;
                var hostPass = _config.hostnameFilter.some(function(host) {
                    return hostname.indexOf(host) > -1;
                });

                return  envPass && hostPass;
            }
        }

        // 重写onerror方法
        var orgError = global.onerror;
        global.onerror = function(msg, url, row, col, error) {
            var _id = msg + ',' + row + ',' + col;
            var errorObj = {
                msg: msg, 
                url: url, 
                row: row, 
                col: col, 
                error: error,
                _id: _id
            }
            if(_config.id) {
                report.filterError(errorObj)
            }
            orgError && orgError.apply(global, arguments);
        }

        var report = global.ErrorJs = {
            // 初始化
            init: function(config) {
                if(E.isObject(config)) {
                    _config = E.extend(_config, config);
                }
                return report;
            },
            sendError: function(errorType, url, data) {
                data = E.extend(data, report.extraParams);
                if(global.sa) {
                    global.sa(errorType, url, data);
                }
            },
            filterError: function(errorObj) {
                var errorBaseData = {
                    page_type: '',
                    browser_ua: '',
                    client_url: errorObj.url,
                    error_type: 'script_error',
                    error_data: errorObj.msg,
                    error_col: errorObj.col,
                    error_row: errorObj.row,
                    error_stack: errorObj.error && errorObj.error.stack || '',
                }
                if(E.shouldIgnore(errorObj.msg)) return;
                if(E.isRepeat(errorObj._id)) return;
                if(E.isRandomPassed()) return;
                if(!E.filterEnvAndHost()) return;

                report.sendError('error', _config.url, errorBaseData);
            },
            extraParams: {
                page_type: '', // 页面类型
                page_type_detail: '' // 页面类型详情
            },
            
            __onerror__: orgError
        }

        return report;
    }(window))

    var ignoreArr = [/Script error/i, /\$ is not defined/i, /Vue is not defined/i, /transformImg/i, /UET/, /"Vue" is not defined/i];

    ErrorJs.init({
        id: 1,
        url: 'https://www.srmdata.com/unusual',
        ignore: ignoreArr,
        random: 1,
        repeat: 1,
    })

    ErrorJs.extraParams.page_type = window.pageTypeForMonitor || 'other';
</script>
<script>
    // 环境变量 localhost/debug/gray/production
    var NODE_SERVER_ENV = 'production';

    var PMonitor = (function(global) {
        if(global.PMonitor) return PMonitor;

        var _config = {
            url: 'https://www.srmdata.com/performance', // 上报 接口
            random: 1, // 抽样 0-1 1为全量
            delay: 1000, // 延迟上报
            timingOptions: {}, // 可以选择不上报部分字段
            needResourceTiming: false,
            resourceTimingOption: {
                type: ['xmlhttprequest', 'img', 'link', 'css', 'script', 'iframe'], // 默认的资源类型
                timeLimit: 10000, // 需要追踪的慢资源加载的最少毫秒数
            },
            hostnameFilter: ['shein.com', 'shein.co', 'shein.in', 'shein.tw', 'shein.se', 'sheinoutlet.com', 'emmacloth.com', 'makemechic.com', 'romwe.com', 'romwe.co'],
            envFilter: ['localhost', 'debug', 'gray']
        };

        var P = {
            extend: function(target, source) {
                for(var key in source) {
                    target[key] = source[key];
                }
                return target;
            },
            isPlainObject: function(value) {
                return Object.prototype.toString.call(value) === '[object Object]';
            },
            isFalse: function(value) {
                return value === false;
            },
            isRandomPassed: function() {
                return Math.random() >= _config.random;
            },
            filterEnvAndHost: function() {
                var hostname = '';
                if(global.location) hostname = global.location.hostname;
                // 本地/测试/灰度环境不上报，不在监控域名内的不上报
                var envPass = _config.envFilter.indexOf(NODE_SERVER_ENV) === -1;
                var hostPass = _config.hostnameFilter.some(function(host) {
                    return hostname.indexOf(host) > -1;
                });
                
                return  envPass && hostPass;
            },
            // 处理时间过滤
            timingLimitHandle: function(timingKey) {
                // 对计算的时间进行过滤，只保留数值0-100s内的数据
                for(var key in timingKey) {
                    var num = timingKey[key];
                    if(!num || num > 100000 || num < 0) {
                        timingKey[key] = 0;
                    }
                }
                return timingKey;
            },
            // 异常数据过滤
            fieldLimitHandle: function(value, min, max) {
                if(value < min) return 0
            }
        }

        var monitor = global.PMonitor = {
            init: function(config) {
                if(P.isPlainObject(config)) {
                    _config = P.extend(_config, config);
                }
                monitor.bindEvent();
                return monitor;
            },
            // 在onload事件里面绑定性能分析任务
            bindEvent: function() {
                var delay = _config.delay;
                var oriOnload = global.onload;
                global.onload = function(e) {
                    if(typeof oriOnload === 'function') oriOnload(e);
                    if(global.requestIdleCallback) {
                        global.requestIdleCallback(monitor.beforeSendReport, {
                            timeout: delay
                        })
                    }else {
                        setTimeout(monitor.beforeSendReport, delay);
                    }
                }
            },
            // 发送前的性能分析任务
            beforeSendReport: function() {
                // 根据设置的比例，采样上报
                if(P.isRandomPassed()) return;
                if(global.performance) {
                    var oriPerformance = monitor.__oriPerformance__ = global.performance;
                    var timing = oriPerformance.timing;
                    var baseData = {
                        timing: monitor.calculateTiming(timing),
                    }
                    
                    // 判断环境是否上报
                    if(!P.filterEnvAndHost()) return;
    
                    if(_config.needResourceTiming) baseData.resource_timing = monitor.calculateResourceTiming();
                    
                    // 获取页面埋设的信息
                    PMonitor.extraParams.page_type = window.pageTypeForMonitor || 'other';
                    
                    monitor.sendData('performance', _config.url, baseData);
                }
            },
            // 资源加载数据的获取
            calculateResourceTiming: function() {
                var type = _config.resourceTimingOption.type || [];
                var timeLimit = _config.resourceTimingOption.timeLimit;
                var resourceData = performance.getEntries();
                data = resourceData.filter(function(resource) {
                    return type.indexOf(resource.initiatorType) > -1;
                });

                return {
                    slow_resource: monitor.trackSlowResource(data, timeLimit),
                }
            },
            // 追踪加载慢的资源
            trackSlowResource: function(resource, timeLimit) {
                var slowResource = resource.filter(function(r) {
                    return r.duration > timeLimit;
                });
                return slowResource.map(function(r) {
                    return {
                        duration: parseInt(r.duration),
                        url: r.name,
                        type: r.initiatorType,
                        size: r.encodedBodySize
                    }
                })
            },
            // 借用 sa 中的数据发送方法
            sendData: function(errorType, url, data) {
                data = P.extend(data, monitor.extraParams);
                if(global.sa) {
                    global.sa(errorType, url, data);
                }
            },
            // 计算timing指标
            calculateTiming: function (timing) {
                var timingOptions = _config.timingOptions;
                var paintTiming = monitor.calcultePaintTiming('paint');
                var timingKey = {
                    // 准备新页面耗时
                    prdt: timing.fetchStart - timing.navigationStart || 0,
                    // DNS解析时间
                    dnst: timing.domainLookupEnd - timing.domainLookupStart || 0,  
                    // TCP建立时间
                    tcpt: timing.connectEnd - timing.connectStart || 0, 
                    // request请求耗时
                    reqt: timing.responseEnd - timing.requestStart || 0, 
                    // 白屏时间, TTFB
                    wipt: timing.responseStart - timing.navigationStart || 0, 
                    // html文档解析时间
                    doct:timing.domComplete - timing.domLoading || 0,
                    // dom解析时间
                    domt: timing.domContentLoadedEventEnd - timing.domContentLoadedEventStart || 0,
                    // 整个页面加载时间
                    lodt: timing.loadEventEnd - timing.navigationStart || 0,
                    // onload事件执行时间
                    onlt: timing.loadEventEnd - timing.loadEventStart || 0,
                    // 上个页面卸载时间
                    pult: timing.unloadEventEnd - timing.unloadEventStart || 0,
                    // 重定向时间
                    rdit: timing.redirectEnd - timing.redirectStart || 0,
                    // 用户可交互时间
                    ttit: timing.domContentLoadedEventEnd - timing.navigationStart || 0,
                    // 首屏渲染时间
                    fplt: monitor.fplt || 0,
                    // node服务器请求api链路时间
                    nlkt: monitor.nlkt || 0,
                    // first-paint 首次渲染
                    fppt: paintTiming.fppt || 0,
                    // first-contentful-paint 首次内容渲染
                    fcpt: paintTiming.fcpt || 0,
                    // 预留字段，暂无特殊意义
                    rdf1: 0,
                    rdf2: 0,
                    rdf3: 0,
                }
                // 根据配置删除禁止上报的字段
                for(var key in timingOptions) {
                    if(P.isFalse(timingOptions[key])) delete timingKey[key];
                }
                // 对可能产生的负值情况进行过滤
                timingKey = P.timingLimitHandle(timingKey)   
                return timingKey;
            },
            calcultePaintTiming: function(name) {
                var performanceEntries = [];
                var fppt = 0;
                var fcpt = 0;

                if(global.performance.getEntriesByType) {
                    performanceEntries = global.performance.getEntriesByType('paint');
                }
                performanceEntries.forEach(function(value) {
                    if(value.name === 'first-paint') fppt = Math.round(value.startTime);
                    if(value.name === 'first-contentful-paint') fcpt = Math.round(value.startTime);
                })
                return {
                    fppt: fppt,
                    fcpt: fcpt
                };
            },
            extraParams: {
                page_type: '', // 页面类型
                page_type_detail: '' // 页面类型详情
            },

            // 每次调用window.performance得到的对象存在此处
            __oriPerformance__: null
        }

        return monitor;
    }(window))

    PMonitor.init({
        url: 'https://www.srmdata.com/performance',
        random: 1,
    })

</script>

<script>
        var S_Module = S_Module || {};
        (function(){
            var modules = {};
            //定义
            S_Module.define = function(name, obj){
                modules[name] = obj;
            };
            //引用模块
            S_Module.require = function(name){
                if( modules[name] )
                return modules[name];
                // else
                // console.error('模块 ' + name + ' 加载失败');
            };
            S_Module.initModule = function(arr, opts){
                opts = opts || {};
                for( var i=0; i<arr.length; i++ ){
                    var name = arr[i];
                    var module = modules[name];
                    if( module ){
                        module.initialize && module.initialize(opts);
                    } else {
                        // console.error('模块 ' + name + ' 加载失败');
                    }
                }
            };
        })();
    </script>

<script>
    // 列表页 pv提前发送，待优化
    var getABTInfoByPosKey =(function(){
        var ABTInfo = {};
        var ABTCookie = '';
        var ABTInfoEmpty = {poskey:'',abtexp:'',abtbranch:''};
        return function(_posKey,_abt){
            if(_abt){
                return {
                    'poskey':_abt.poskey||'',
                    'abtexp':_abt.abtexp||'',
                    'abtbranch':_abt.abtbranch||'',
                    'p':_abt.p||'',
                    'type':_abt.type||'',
                }
            }
            if(!_posKey){
                return ABTInfoEmpty;
            }
            if(_posKey == "refreshCookie"){//指令 刷新
                ABTCookie = '';
                ABTInfo = {};
                return;
            }
            if(!ABTCookie){
                //get cookie
                var parts = ("; " + document.cookie).split("; abt-info=");
                if (parts.length == 2)
                    ABTCookie = decodeURIComponent(parts.pop().split(";").shift())
                if(!ABTCookie){
                    return ABTInfoEmpty;
                }
                //解析成 ABTInfo
                var ABTArray = ABTCookie.split('|');
                for(var i = 0;i<ABTArray.length;i++){
                    var item = ABTArray[i].split('~');
                    if(item.length>=3){//合法
                        ABTInfo[item[0]] = {
                            poskey : item[0],
                            abtexp : item[1],
                            abtbranch : item[2],
                            p: (item[4]||'').replace(/`/g,'|'),
                            type: item[3]||'',
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return ABTInfo[_posKey] ? ABTInfo[_posKey] : ABTInfoEmpty;
        }
    })();

    var gbExposeTraceid
    ;!function() {
        var traceidComponents = {}

        //购物流程 traceid 追踪
        gbExposeTraceid = function(type, payload) {
            try {
                var traceids = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('saTraceids'))
                if(!traceids) {
                    traceids = {}
                }
                var defaultTraceid = 'spc`' + Date.now() + '`'+ (window.gbGetCookie('cookieId') || '')
                var traceid =''
                if(type == 'setComponent') {
                    if(!traceidComponents[payload.componentName]) {
                        traceid = payload.traceid || defaultTraceid
                        if(payload.params){
                            traceid +='`'+payload.params
                        }
                        traceidComponents[payload.componentName] = traceid
                    }else{
                        traceid = traceidComponents[payload.componentName]
                    }
                } else if(type == 'getComponent') {
                    if(!traceidComponents[payload.componentName]){
                        traceidComponents[payload.componentName] = defaultTraceid
                    }
                    traceid = traceidComponents[payload.componentName]
                } else if(type == 'setProduct') {
                    traceid = payload.traceid || defaultTraceid
                    if(payload.params){
                        traceid +='`'+payload.params
                    }
                    traceids[payload.goods_id] = traceid
                } else if(type == 'getProduct') {
                    traceid = traceids[payload.goods_id]
                }
                sessionStorage.setItem('saTraceids', JSON.stringify(traceids))
                return traceid
            } catch (error) {
                console.log(error);
            }
        }
    }();
    var gbAddBagTraceFrom
    ;!function() {
        //追踪加车来源
        gbAddBagTraceFrom = function(type, payload) {
            var tracefrom = null
            var addBagFrom = ''
            try {
                tracefrom = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('saAddBagTraceFrom')) || {}
                if(type == 'setProductFrom') {
                    tracefrom[payload.goods_id] = payload.from || 'other'
                } else if(type == 'getProductFrom') {
                    addBagFrom = tracefrom[payload.goods_id] || 'other'
                }
                sessionStorage.setItem('saAddBagTraceFrom', JSON.stringify(tracefrom))
                return addBagFrom
            } catch (error) {
                console.log(error);
            }
        }
    }();
    var gbGetCookie = function(name) {
        var value = "; " + document.cookie;
        var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
            return decodeURIComponent(parts.pop().split(";").shift())
        } else {
            return undefined;
        }
    }

    var gbGetQueryString = function(name) {
        //get url query params
        var reg = new RegExp('(^|&)' + name + '=([^&]*)(&|$)', 'i')
        var r = window.location.search.substr(1).match(reg)
        if (r != null) return unescape(r[2])
        return null
    }
    window.getABTInfoByPosKey = getABTInfoByPosKey
    window.gbExposeTraceid = gbExposeTraceid
    window.gbGetCookie = gbGetCookie
    window.gbGetQueryString = gbGetQueryString
    window.gbAddBagTraceFrom = gbAddBagTraceFrom
</script>

<script>
    (function() {
        var setCookie = function(name, val, expires, domain) {
            /*setCookie start*/
            var __CurrentMainDomain__ = (function() {
                var hostnameArray = location.hostname.split('.')
                return '.' + hostnameArray.slice(-2).join('.')
            })()

            //domain = domain || __CurrentMainDomain__;
            var text = String(encodeURIComponent(val))
            var date = new Date()
            date.setTime(date.getTime() + Number(expires) * 1000)
            text += '; expires=' + date.toUTCString()
            // domain
            text += '; path=/'
            if (typeof domain != 'undefined' && domain != '') {
                text += '; domain=' + __CurrentMainDomain__
            }

            document.cookie = name + '=' + text
        }
        var getCookie = function(name) {
            var value = "; " + document.cookie;
            var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
            if (parts.length >= 2) {
                return decodeURIComponent(parts.pop().split(";").shift())
            } else {
                return undefined;
            }
        }
        if (location.pathname == '/') {
            setCookie('cate_active_name', 0, 3600 * 24 * 30)
            setCookie('cate_channel_type', $('.j-nav-first-cate-0').data('channel_type'), 3600 * 24 * 30)
        }
        var cate_active_name = getCookie('cate_active_name')
        document.querySelector('.j-nav2-ctn-holder').style.display = 'none'
        if (cate_active_name && document.querySelector('.j-nav-first-cate-' + cate_active_name) && document.querySelector('.j-nav2-ctn-' + cate_active_name)) {
            document.querySelector('.j-nav-first-cate-' + cate_active_name).className += ' cate-active'
            document.querySelector('.j-nav2-ctn-' + cate_active_name).style.display = 'table-cell'
        } else if (document.querySelector('.j-nav-first-cate-0') && document.querySelector('.j-nav2-ctn-0')) {
            document.querySelector('.j-nav-first-cate-0').className += ' cate-active'
            document.querySelector('.j-nav2-ctn-0').style.display = 'table-cell'
        }
    })()
</script>

    GB_webp_url.forEach(function(item) {
        if (img.indexOf(item) > -1) {
            img = img.replace(/\b(.jpg)\b/, ".webp")
            return
        }
    })
    return img
}

      function getCookie(name) {//取最新的cookie
          var value = "; " + document.cookie;
          var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
          if (parts.length >= 2) {

      function checkSameCookie(objName){
        var arrStr = document.cookie.split("; "),count=0;
        for (var i = 0; i < arrStr.length; i++) {
          var temp = arrStr[i].split("=");
          if (temp[0] == objName) {
            count++
          }
        }
        return count>1;
      }

  function getCookie(name){/*获取指定名称的cookie的值*/

        setTheme: function (theme) {
            // return
            if (!theme) return

            _config_.oper_name = theme.content && theme.content[0] ? theme.content[0].oper_name : ''

    // 构建ccc埋点sa参数
    function buildSaParamWithCCC(config, act_id, jumpType) {
        if (!config) return {}
        var page_id = SaPageInfo.page_id || ''
        var scene_id = config.scene_id || 0
        var scene_name = config.scene_name || 0
        var oper_id = config.oper_id || 0
        var tag_id = SHEIN_W.getCookie('pwa_user_email') ? '50000' : '50001'
        var content_list = window.location.href

        var act_id = act_id || ''
        var jumpType = jumpType || 0
        //          页面ID`    tab-ID`场景ID_场景类型`         模板ID`区块ID`运营位ID`运营位位置ID`样式ID`人群ID`坑位ID`热区ID`层级ID`跳转类型ID 
        var spm = page_id + '`0`' + scene_id + '_' + scene_name + '`0`0`' + oper_id + '`1`1`' + tag_id + '`0`0`0`' + jumpType

        var saParam = {
            spm: spm,
            act_id: act_id,
            content_list: content_list,
            abtest: '-`-`-',
        }
        return saParam
    }

<script>
    ; (function () {
        function setCookie(name, val, expires, domain) {
            //domain = domain || __CurrentMainDomain__;
            var text = String(encodeURIComponent(val));
            var date = new Date();
            date.setTime(date.getTime() + Number(expires) * 1000);
            text += '; expires=' + date.toUTCString();
            // domain
            text += '; path=/';
            if (typeof (domain) != "undefined" && domain != "") {
                text += '; domain=' + __CurrentMainDomain__;
            }

            document.cookie = name + '=' + text;
        }

        function getCookie(name) {/*获取指定名称的cookie的值*/
            var value = "; " + document.cookie;
            var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
            if (parts.length >= 2) {
                return decodeURIComponent(parts.pop().split(";").shift())
            } else {
                return undefined;
            }
        }

        function jumpToMx() {
            //jump to mx
            var thisURL = (document.URL);//获取当前页面链接
            var newURL = ''
            if (thisURL.indexOf('es.localhost') > -1) {
                // 开发环境 (需要给死一个ip地址)
                newURL = thisURL.replace('es.localhost', 'mx.localhost')
            } else if (thisURL.indexOf('pc7-es.shein.com') > -1) {
                // 测试环境
                newURL = thisURL.replace('pc7-es.shein.com', 'pc7-mx.shein.com')
            } else {
                newURL = thisURL.replace('es.shein.com', 'www.shein.com.mx');
            }

            newURL.indexOf('?') < 0 ? newURL += '?' : newURL += '&';
            // shein.com 和 shein.com.mx不能互相访问cookie!
            location.href = newURL + 'ref=es&ret=mx&es_to_mx=1';
        }

        // es跳mx
        var blang = (navigator.language || navigator.browserLanguage).toLowerCase();
        var lang = 'vn';
        if (lang === 'es' && blang.indexOf('es') > -1) {
            var country = getCookie('country');

            if (/es_to_mx=0/.test(location.href)) {
				// 跳回来之后设置cookie,防止下次西班牙站点还会跳
				setCookie('es_to_mx', '0', 3600*24)
            }
            var es_to_mx = getCookie('es_to_mx');
            // 为0说明1天内不需要跳转
            if (es_to_mx !== '0') {
                if (!country) {
                    console.log('')
                    $.ajax({
                        type: "POST",
                        url: "/judgeIP",
                        success: function (res) {
                            if (0 == res.code) {
                                country = res.info.location.country;
                                setCookie('country', country, 3600 * 24 * 30, true);
                                if (country.toLowerCase() === 'mx') {
                                    jumpToMx();
                                    // setCookie('es_to_mx', 1, 3600 * 24 * 30, true);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    });
                } else if (country.toLowerCase() === 'mx') {
                    jumpToMx();
                }
            }
        }


        function currencyAutoMatch(country) {
            //中东国家默认货币
            // 1.ar和mar站根据ip，匹配对应国家的币种，在用户后首次打开时即显示匹配后的币种。
            // 2.若用户已经保存有货币，则不需要重新判断和更改
            var currencyMap = {
                SA: 'SAR',
                AE: 'AED',
                KW: 'KWD',
                QA: 'QAR',
                OM: 'OMR',
                BH: 'BHD',
            }, site = 'vn',
                currency = getCookie('m_currency_' + site)

            if ((site == 'ar') && !currency && country in currencyMap) {
                currency = currencyMap[country]
                $.get('/changeCurrency?currency=' + currency, function () {
                    setCookie('m_currency_' + site, currency, 60 * 60 * 24 * 7)
                    window.location.reload(true)
                })
            }
        }

        //cookie中countryId不存在并且是非国际站
        if (getCookie('countryId') == undefined && 'vn' != 'en') {
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: '/judgeIP',
                success: function (res) {
                    if (res.code != 0) return
                    var country = res.info.location.country;
                    var countryId = res.info.location.country_id;
                    setCookie('countryId', countryId, 3600 * 24 * 30, false)

                    currencyAutoMatch(country)
                }
            })
        }
    }())
</script>

    ! function (f, b, e, v, n, t, s) {
        if (f.fbq) return;
        n = f.fbq = function () {
            n.callMethod ?
                n.callMethod.apply(n, arguments) : n.queue.push(arguments)
        };
        if (!f._fbq) f._fbq = n;
        n.push = n;
        n.loaded = !0;
        n.version = '2.0';
        n.queue = [];
        t = b.createElement(e);
        t.async = !0;
        t.src = v;
        s = b.getElementsByTagName(e)[0];
        s.parentNode.insertBefore(t, s)
    }(window, document, 'script', 'https://connect.facebook.net/en_US/fbevents.js');
    fbq('init', '992946534136988');

            if (d.getElementById(id)) {
                return;
            }
            js = d.createElement(s);

        }(document, 'script', 'facebook-jssdk'));
    


    window.doFbq = function (action, params) {
        ! function () {
            if (typeof Object.assign != 'function') {
                Object.assign = function (target) {
                    'use strict';
                    if (target == null) {
                        throw new TypeError('Cannot convert undefined or null to object');
                    }

                    target = Object(target);
                    for (var index = 1; index < arguments.length; index++) {
                        var source = arguments[index];
                        if (source != null) {
                            for (var key in source) {
                                if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(source, key)) {
                                    target[key] = source[key];
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    return target;
                };
            }
            if (typeof fbq !== 'undefined') {
                var _form = Object.assign({}, {
                    content_type: 'product', //直接上报此文本
                    value: 1.00, //固定上报此价格
                    currency: 'USD' //固定上报此币种
                }, params)

                if(typeof fbq.init !== 'function') {
                    var _timeIndex = 0;
                    var _timeId = setInterval(function() {
                        _timeIndex++;
                        if(typeof fbq.init == 'function' || _timeIndex>5) {
                            fbq('track', action, _form);
                            clearInterval(_timeId);
                        }
                    }, 1000);
                } else {
                    fbq('track', action, _form);
                }

            }
        }();
    }

<script>
  var GB_Login_analysis_obj = {
    _clickStatus: {
      plus: false,
    },
    clickLoading: function (type) {
      var self = this
      if(self._clickStatus[type]) {
        return true
      } else {
        self._clickStatus[type] = true 
        setTimeout(function() { 
          self._clickStatus[type] = false 
        }, 300)
        return false
      }
    },
    loginGAEventSend: function(json) {
        var statusLabel = json.value ? 'Success' : 'Fail-';
        var source = json.source || ''
        var _msg = '';
        if (json && json.failMsg) {
          _msg = json.val ? '' : json.failMsg
        }
        var para = {
          eventCategory: '登录注册页',
          eventAction: json.action||this.getEventAction(json),
          eventLabel: statusLabel + _msg,
          transport: 'beacon',
        }
        if(typeof json.value != 'undefined'){
          para.eventValue = json.value
        }
        if(json.hasLabel){
          para.eventLabel = json.eventLabel||this.getEventLabel(json);
        }
        ga('shein.send', 'event', para);
        if(json.type == 'Sign'&& source !="liveChat"){
          // sa('send',{'activity_name':'click_signin','activity_param':''});
        }else if(json.type == 'Register'&& source !="liveChat"){
          var preferences = '';
          if(json.prepferences && json.prepferences.length){
            for(var i = 0;i<json.prepferences.length;i++){
              preferences += json.prepferences[i]+',';
            }
            if(preferences.length>0){preferences = preferences.substring(0,preferences.length-1)}
          }
          // sa('send',{'activity_name':'click_register','activity_param':{style_preference:preferences}});
        }
        
    },
    getEventAction:function(json){
       var type = json.type;
       var el = $(".j-login-con");
       var source = json.source||el.attr("data-login-source");
       var cate = "";
       if(type=="Sign"){
          cate+="Sign In"
       }else if(type=="Register"){
          cate+="Register"
       }
       if(source=="loginPage"){
          var queryStringObj = SHEIN_W.queryString.parse(window.location.search)
          cate+=queryStringObj.direction == 'nav' ? " - Login" : " - Other"
       }else if(source=="other"){
          cate+=" - Other"
       }else if(source=="checkout"){
         cate+=" - Checkout"
       }else if(source=="special-coupon"){
          cate+=" - 专题-领券"
       }else if(source=="special-luckyDraw"){
          cate+=" - 专题-抽奖"
       }else if(source == 'liveChat'){
        cate+=" - liveChat"
       } else if (source === 'freeTrial') {
        cate+=" - FreeTrial"
       } else if (source == 'wishlist' || source == 'save') {
        cate+=" - Wishlist"   
       } else {
        cate+=" - Other"
       }
       return cate;
    },
    SelectPlusSize: function(val) {
      if(typeof(ga) == 'function') {
        ga('shein.send',{
            hitType: 'event',
            eventCategory: '登录注册页',
            eventAction: 'SelectPlusSize',
            eventLabel: val
        });
      }
    },
    getEventLabel:function(json){
      var loginType = json.loginType
      var el = $(".j-login-con");
      var source = json.source ||el.attr("data-login-source");
      var cate = "";
      if(source=="loginPage"){
        cate=" - login"
      }else if(source=="other"){
        cate=" - other"
      }else if(source=="checkout"){
        cate=" - checkout"
      }else if(source=="special-coupon"){
        cate=" - 专题-领券"
      }else if(source=="special-luckyDraw"){
        cate=" - 专题-抽奖"
      }else if(source == 'liveChat'){
        cate=" - liveChat"
      } else if (source === 'freeTrial') {
        cate+=" - FreeTrial"
       } else if (source == 'wishlist' || source == 'save') {
        cate+=" - Wishlist"   
       }
      return loginType+cate;
    },
    bindEvent: function () {
      var self = this
      $(document).on("click",".j-login-con",function(e) {
           var $target = $(e.target);
           var source = $target.closest(".j-login-con").attr("data-login-source")
           var para=null;
          if($target.hasClass('j-a-forgot-psd')) {
              para ={
                eventCategory:"登录注册页",
                eventAction:"ClickForgotYourPassword"
              }
          }else if($target.closest(".j-btn-facebook").length){
              var fbLabel = GB_Login_analysis_obj.getEventLabel( {source:source,loginType:"Facebook"})
              var direction = SHEIN_W.getQueryString('direction') || ''
              if (source === 'loginPage') {
                if (direction === 'nav' || location.search === '') fbLabel = 'Facebook - Login'
                else fbLabel = 'Facebook - Other'
              }
             para = {
              eventCategory: '登录注册页',
              eventAction: "ClickFacebook",
              eventLabel: fbLabel
            }
          
          }else if($target.closest(".j-btn-google").length){
			  var googleLabel = GB_Login_analysis_obj.getEventLabel({source:source,loginType:"Google"})
			  var direction = SHEIN_W.getQueryString('direction') || ''
              if (source === 'loginPage') {
                if (direction === 'nav' || location.search === '') googleLabel = 'Google - Login'
                else googleLabel = 'Google - Other'
              }
            para = {
              eventCategory: '登录注册页',
              eventAction: "ClickGoogle",
              eventLabel: googleLabel
            }
          }else if($target.closest(".j-btn-vk").length){
            var vkLabel = GB_Login_analysis_obj.getEventLabel({source:source,loginType:"VK"})
            var direction = SHEIN_W.getQueryString('direction') || ''
            if (source === 'loginPage') {
                if (direction === 'nav' || location.search === '') vkLabel = 'VK - Login'
                else vkLabel = 'Vk - Other'
            }
            para = {
              eventCategory: '登录注册页',
              eventAction: "ClickVK",
              eventLabel: vkLabel
            }
          }
          if(para!=null){
            ga('shein.send', 'event', para);
          }
      }).on("click",".j-plus-size-item",function(e) {
        if(self.clickLoading('plus')) return
        self.SelectPlusSize($(this).data('plus'))
      })
    },
    getPageFrom:function(){
      var obj={formPage:'',params:{}};
      var queryStringObj = SHEIN_W.queryString.parse(window.location.search);
      if(queryStringObj){
        if(queryStringObj.pageFrom){
          obj.formPage = queryStringObj.pageFrom
        }
        if(queryStringObj.params){
          obj.params = SHEIN_W.queryString.parse(queryStringObj.params)
        }
      }
      return obj;
    }
  }
  GB_Login_analysis_obj.bindEvent()

  if("vn" == "es"){
     $(document).on("click", '.j-newto-shein input[type="email"]', function(e) {
      if(typeof(ga) == 'function'){
        ga('shein.send',{
            hitType: 'event',
            eventCategory: '登录注册页',
            eventAction: 'EditEmailAddress'
        })
      }
    })
  }
</script>

<script data-id="index-stylegallery-vue" type="text/javascript">
	Vue.component('index-stylegallery', {
	    template: '#index-stylegallery',
	    data: function() {
	    	return {
	    		goods: {},
                navData: [],
	    		status: { loading: -1, loginModal: false},
	    		quickOpenEvent: { status: { open: false } },
                language: "vn",
				loginModal: {type: '', ref: null},
				forgetPassword: { showForm: false, },
                gaCache: {},
                added_wish: {}
	    	}
	    },
	    computed: {
        },
        watch: {
        },
        mounted: function() {
            this.bindEvent();
			if (typeof GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance != 'undefined') {
				GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance.update()
			}
        },
	    methods: {
	    	bindEvent: function() {
                var self = this;
                $( function(){
                    self.loadGoodsData();
                    self.getNavData();
                    }
                )

                $(window).scroll(function(e){
                    if(self.scrollTimeout){
                        clearTimeout(self.scrollTimeout);
                    }
                    self.scrollTimeout = setTimeout(self.sa_scrollHandler,500);
                });
	    	},
	    	loadGoodsData: function() {
	    		var self = this;
	    		// $.getJSON('//api-shein.shein.com/review/top_picks?p=1&callback=?', {language: 'vn'}, function(data) {
                $.getJSON('//api-shein.shein.com/social_wear/wear_list_pc?ps=10&callback=?', {
                    language: gbCalcSocialWearLang()
                }, function(data) {
			        if(data && data.info && data.info.wear_img_list.length) {
			            self.goods = data.info.wear_img_list;
			            self.nextTick();
                        $('.outfit-container').css('visibility', 'visible');
                        $(window).scroll();
			        } else {
			        	$('.outfit-container').hide();
			        }
			    });
	    	},
	    	// opt
            quickViewOpen: function(opt) {
            	var self = this;
            	if(this.status.loading >= 0) return;
            	this.status.loading = opt.index;
                this.sa_clickPhoto(opt.goodsId,opt.index+1);
                this.quickOpenEvent = {
                    status: {
                        open: true,
                        pageType: 'home'
                    },
                    data: {
                        goodsId: opt.goodsId,
                        styleGalleryData: {
                            _oimg: opt.oimg || '',
                            _simg: opt.simg || '',
                            pageType: 'styleGallery-home'
                        },
                        index: opt.index
                    },
                    callback: function(data, code) {
                        self.quickOpenEvent = { status: { open: false } };
                    	self.status.loading = -1;
                    }
                }
            },
            qvAddWishSuc: function(goods_id) {
                this.$set(this.added_wish, goods_id, 1)
            },
            //DOM渲染完成后数据处理函数
            nextTick: function() {
            	var self = this;
            	var _prevButton = '.j-outfit-prev';
	            var _nextButton = '.j-outfit-next';

	            this.$nextTick(function () {
	            	self.outfitSwiper = new Swiper ('.j-outfit-swiper', {
		                direction: 'horizontal',
		                prevButton: _prevButton,
		                nextButton: _nextButton,
		                spaceBetween: 20,
		                slidesPerView: 5,
		                slidesPerGroup : 5,
		                lazyLoadingInPrevNext : true,
		                lazyLoading: true,
                        onSlideChangeEnd: function(swiper){
                            self.sa_exposePhotos();
                            if(typeof(gaEventCenter) != 'undefined') {
                                gaEventCenter.$emit('slideChangeEnd', {swiper: swiper})
                            }
							if (typeof GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance != 'undefined') {
			                    GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance.update()
			                }
                        },
                        onSlidePrevEnd: function(swiper){
                            if(typeof(gaEventCenter) != 'undefined') {
                                gaEventCenter.$emit('SlidePrevEnd')
                            }
                        },
                        onSlideNextEnd: function(swiper){
                            if(typeof(gaEventCenter) != 'undefined') {
                                gaEventCenter.$emit('SlideNextEnd')
                            }
                        }
		            });
					if (typeof GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance != 'undefined') {
						GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance.update()
					}
		        });
            },
            getNavData: function(times) {
                var self = this, navLoadFailTime = 0;
                if(times) navLoadFailTime = times;
                var navAjax = $.ajax({
                    // url: '//api-shein.shein.com/review/m/category',
                    url: '//api-shein.shein.com/social_wear/label_list_pc',
                    type: 'get',
                    dataType: 'jsonp',
                    jsonp: 'callback',
                    timeout: 5000,
                    data: {
                        language: gbCalcSocialWearLang(),
                        label_type:1
                    },
                })
                .done(function(data) {
                    if(data && data.code == 0 && data.info && data.info.category_list.length) {

                        self.navData = data.info.category_list;
                        self.$nextTick(function() {
                            new Swiper ('.j-tags-swiper', {
                                direction: 'horizontal',
                                prevButton: '.j-tags-prev',
                                nextButton: '.j-tags-next',
                                spaceBetween: 15,
                                slidesPerView: 'auto',
                                slidesPerGroup : 5,
                                onSlideChangeEnd: function(){
                                    self.sa_exposeMenu();
									if (typeof GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance != 'undefined') {
					                    GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance.update()
					                }
                                }
                            });
							if (typeof GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance != 'undefined') {
								GB_SHEIN_lazyLoadInstance.update()
							}
                        })
                        $(window).scroll();
                    }
                })
                .fail(function() {
                    navLoadFailTime++;
                    if(navLoadFailTime < 5) {
                        self.getNavData(navLoadFailTime);
                    }
                })
            },
            clickNav: function(opt) {
                if(opt.url) {
                    window.location.href = opt.url;
                    this.sa_clickMenu(opt.labelId,opt.pitPos);
                }
            },
			// 登录弹框
			openLoginModal: function(cbType, cbRef) {
				this.status.loginModal = true;
				this.loginModal.type = cbType;
				this.loginModal.ref = cbRef;
			},
			loginModalSucCb: function() {
				this.status.loginModal = false;
				SHEIN_W.userModule.update_top_login();
				cart_module.reload();
				if (this.loginModal.type === 'qv-add-wish') {
					this.loginModal.ref.addToWish();
				}
			},
            loginModalClose: function() {
                this.status.loginModal = false
                GBGaSend({
                    category: '登录注册页',
                    action: 'ClosePopUps',
                    label: 'Sign/Register-AddToWishlist'
                })
            },
            transformImg: transformImg,
            sa_scrollHandler:function(){
                //style gallery 曝光
                var stylegalleryMenu = $('.c-style-gallery-nav');
                if(!stylegalleryMenu.hasClass("sa_expose")){
                    if($(window).height() >= stylegalleryMenu.get(0).getBoundingClientRect().bottom * 0.8){
                        this.sa_exposeMenu();
                        this.sa_exposePhotos();
                        stylegalleryMenu.addClass('sa_expose');
                    }
                }
            },
            sa_exposeMenu:function(){
                var stylegalleryMenu = $('.c-style-gallery-nav');
                var clientRect = stylegalleryMenu.get(0).getBoundingClientRect();
                var param = '';
                var self = this;
                stylegalleryMenu.find('.swiper-slide').each(function(index,item){
                    var itemClientRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
                    if(itemClientRect.left>=clientRect.left-20 && itemClientRect.right<=clientRect.right+20 && !$(item).hasClass('sa_expose')){
                        param += (index+1)+'`'+self.navData[index].label_id+',';
                        $(item).addClass('sa_expose');
                    }
                });
                if(param.length>0){
                    param = param.substring(0,param.length-1);
                    sa('send',{'activity_name':'expose_home_style_gallery_style','activity_param':{
                        content_list:param
                    }});
                }
            },
            sa_exposePhotos:function(){
                var stylegalleryPhotos = $('.c-style-gallery-nav').prev();
                var clientRect = stylegalleryPhotos.get(0).getBoundingClientRect();
                var param = '';
                var self = this;
                stylegalleryPhotos.find('.swiper-slide').each(function(index,item){
                    var itemClientRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
                    if(itemClientRect.left>=clientRect.left-20 && itemClientRect.right<=clientRect.right+20 && !$(item).hasClass('sa_expose')){
                        param += (index+1)+'`'+self.goods[index].goods_id+',';
                        $(item).addClass('sa_expose');
                    }
                });
                if(param.length>0){
                    param = param.substring(0,param.length-1);
                    sa('send',{'activity_name':'expose_home_style_gallery_goods','activity_param':{
                        goods_list:param,
                    }});
                }
            },
            sa_clickPhoto:function(_goodId,_pitPos){
                sa('send',{'activity_name':'click_home_style_gallery_goods','activity_param':{
                    goods_list:_pitPos+'`'+_goodId,
                }});
            },
            sa_clickMenu:function(_labelId,_pitPos){
                if(typeof _labelId == 'undefined'){
                    _labelId = 0;
                    _pitPos = 1;
                }
                sa('send',{'activity_name':'click_home_style_gallery_style','activity_param':{content_list:_pitPos+'`'+_labelId}});
            }
	    }
	});
</script>
